If a future trace again shows `sl_dlss_g` throwing `_Throw_C_error` right after a startup OFF signal, verify first that the real OFF call is still being suppressed during the startup window.

### 2026-04-12 - Return promoted Streamline startup-handoff Presents through the bypass/original DXGI Present path
- **Root cause refinement**: In the GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_225856`, the previous callback-dormancy fix was clearly in effect: CE promoted only one startup-handoff Present, `DX12: PostSL gated callback deferred until startup transition window expires` fired on the first synthetic callback, wrapper ECL progress kept advancing, and the new early startup-stall dump again captured the same `sl_dlss_g` / `std::_Throw_C_error` family. That narrowed the remaining bug to the top-level handoff Present itself. Source inspection showed why: `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` intentionally let that one Streamline-originated Present escape the synthetic route so CE could run the normal top-level `ProcessFrame` path, but after that processing `DetourPresent` still used the normal `s_slRoutingActive` fast-path and called `oPresent` again. Because the promoted call had already entered from Streamline's external Present chain, CE was feeding the same handoff Present back through Streamline a second time instead of returning through the bypass/original DXGI Present path.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` now exposes `ShouldPreferBypassReturnPathForPromotedStreamlineTopLevelPresent()` so the promoted-handoff return-path rule is explicit and testable.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` now uses that helper in both `DetourPresent` and `DetourPresent1`. When CE promotes a Streamline startup-handoff Present to the top-level live path, it still runs the normal CE bootstrap/routing logic, but on the way out it now prefers the bypass trampoline / original DXGI Present path instead of calling `oPresent` / `oPresent1` and recursively re-entering Streamline's external Present hook.
  3. `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now adds `DXGISharedTest.PromotedStreamlineStartupHandoffPresentPrefersBypassReturnPath`.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime can expose a late startup-handoff Present that must take the top-level CE path exactly once so live routing/bootstrap state can advance. But that does not mean the same call should be re-submitted into Streamline on the way out. The generic safe invariant is: let the selected handoff Present run CE's top-level processing path, then return via the bypass/original DXGI Present path.
- **Verification**:

### 2026-04-12 - Keep the PostSL callback itself dormant through the pure-DLSS startup window
- **Root cause refinement**: In the GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_224959`, the previous two fixes were both clearly in effect: CE only promoted one startup-handoff Present to the top-level live path, deferred-OFF churn now logged `extended startup window` instead of reopening a fresh handoff, and the normal 30-frame `Present STALLED` path again produced a watchdog dump. But the dump still landed in the same `sl_dlss_g` `std::_Throw_C_error` family. The hook trace showed the remaining problem precisely: during the still-active startup transition window, CE entered the first PostSL callback for the pure-DLSS top-level-handoff wrapper-progress family, completed synthetic startup activation, and then spent that same callback on `DX12: PostSL warm-up after reactivation epoch=1 frame=1/15`. No later PostSL callback ever arrived before `Present STALLED`. That means the failure had moved earlier than the later ECL/render deferral checks: simply entering the PostSL callback through Streamline's Present chain during this fragile startup family can already perturb Streamline, even if CE ultimately returns before any real overlay GPU submit.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldDeferPostSLCallbackUntilStartupTransitionWindowExpires()`, which is true only for the pure-DLSS startup-handoff wrapper-progress family while the startup transition window is still active, the one-shot top-level bootstrap has already been consumed, wrapper progress is already visible, and startup is still half-armed.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now checks that policy in `PostSLOverlayRenderGated()` before the callback enters the normal in-flight path. In this startup family CE now skips the callback entirely until the startup window expires instead of entering PostSL just to spend the only decisive callback on warm-up or later deferral logic.
  3. `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now adds `DXGISharedTest.PureDLSSStartupCallbackStaysDormantUntilStartupWindowExpires` so both startup-window layers stay explicit and testable: callback-entry deferral and later render/ECL deferral.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime can expose a startup family where wrapper queue progress proves the path is live, but the runtime still only surfaces one decisive synthetic Present while its internal startup pipeline is fragile. In that family, entering the callback itself can be too early even if later code would have deferred real rendering. The safe invariant is not just "do not submit ECL during the startup window" but also, for this stronger proof family, "do not even enter the PostSL callback until the startup window expires."

### 2026-04-12 - Preserve one-shot startup-handoff bootstrap across startup-window extensions and add early pure-DLSS startup stall dumps
- **Root cause refinement**: In the newer GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_220851`, DLSS FG still froze with the black-window startup family even after the earlier 1500ms startup-window extension and startup-window render deferral fixes. The latest trace exposed a concrete regression in CE itself: the run logged `Treating Streamline startup-handoff Present as top-level live Present #1`, then deferred `OFF` inside the startup window, and then incorrectly logged `Treating Streamline startup-handoff Present as top-level live Present #2` during the same handoff. Source inspection showed why: `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` still reset `streamlineStartupTopLevelPresentConsumed` inside `ArmStreamlineStartupTransitionWindow()`, and `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` was calling that same fresh-handoff helper even when it only meant to extend the existing startup window during deferred-OFF churn. That reopened the one-shot top-level bootstrap path at exactly the wrong time. The same `220851` bundle also produced no dump artifact because the session ended before the later `SetOptions`-driven 30-frame `Present STALLED` detector or the full 30-second freeze watchdog timeout could fire, while wrapper-queue ECL helper traffic kept the generic watchdog heartbeat alive.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` now separates fresh-handshake reset from simple extension: `ArmStreamlineStartupTransitionWindow()` still resets the one-shot startup-handoff latch for a truly fresh handoff, while new `ExtendStreamlineStartupTransitionWindow()` only lengthens the current window and preserves the already-consumed bootstrap state.
  2. `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` now calls `ExtendStreamlineStartupTransitionWindow()` for deferred-OFF startup churn instead of reusing the fresh-handoff arm helper, so OFF deferral can keep the startup window alive without reopening a second promoted top-level startup-handoff Present in the same handoff.
  3. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldRequestImmediateDumpForPureDLSSStartupWrapperOnlyStall()`, and `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` uses it from the authoritative wrapper-ECL-progress path. When the pure-DLSS startup-handoff family has already consumed its one-shot top-level bootstrap, wrapper ECL progress keeps advancing, startup is still half-armed, and top-level `ProcessFrame` has gone dormant for about a second, CE now requests an immediate watchdog dump instead of waiting for a later `SetOptions` poll or the 30-second freeze timeout.
  4. `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now covers both new invariants: extending the startup transition window must not clear the consumed top-level bootstrap latch, and the pure-DLSS wrapper-only startup stall dump policy only fires for the intended half-armed startup family.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime can defer OFF inside a still-active startup handoff, and extending that handoff window must not be treated as a brand-new handoff that reopens the one-shot top-level Present escape hatch. Likewise, any runtime that transitions into a black-window startup stall after the initial top-level bootstrap but before later polling-based stall detectors run still benefits from an earlier dump trigger based on authoritative wrapper-only forward progress plus vanished top-level Present traffic.

### 2026-04-12 - Extend startup transition window through multi-device DLSS FG OFF->ON->OFF->ON churn
- **Root cause refinement**: In the GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_214313`, DLSS FG still froze with the same `sl_dlss_g` `std::_Throw_C_error` signature even after the prior layered warm-up, signal-coherence, and PostSL submit fixes. The specific failure this time had three contributing factors: (1) the startup transition window (500ms) expired before DLSS FG's multi-device OFF->ON->OFF->ON churn finished — the second ON arrived at +1.28s but the window expired at +0.88s; (2) the `useTopLevelHandoffWrapperProgress` bypass in `ShouldDeferPostSLRenderingDuringStartupTransitionWindow()` let PostSL skip the startup-window ECL deferral, so PostSL activated and started warm-up during SL's fragile multi-device initialization; (3) the warm-up callback fired through SL's Present chain while SL was still churning, likely contributing to SL's internal threading race.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` now calls `DXGIShared::ArmStreamlineStartupTransitionWindow()` when `deferredOffDuringStartupWindow` is true, extending the startup window by another full duration on each deferred OFF signal. This keeps the window active through the entire churn sequence.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` increases `kStreamlineStartupTransitionGraceMs` from 500 to 1500ms, covering the longer churn windows observed on multi-device DLSS FG configurations.
  3. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` removes the `useTopLevelHandoffWrapperProgress` bypass from `ShouldDeferPostSLRenderingDuringStartupTransitionWindow()`. Wrapper ECL progress proves queue topology stability but not that SL's internal pipeline has settled. All families now defer PostSL rendering until the startup window expires or PostSL has already confirmed stable rendering.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime on a multi-device configuration can exhibit OFF->ON->OFF->ON churn during startup that takes longer than 500ms. A startup transition window that expires mid-churn removes CE's protective gating at the worst possible time. Wrapper ECL progress is necessary but not sufficient evidence that the FG pipeline is safe for overlay work.
- **Verification**:

### 2026-04-12 - Restore conservative first-epoch PostSL warm-up and keep deferred Streamline runtime state coherent
- **Root cause refinement**: In the newer GTA V Enhanced freeze bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_200851`, CE finally reached the real `DX12: Post-SL overlay SUBMIT #1` after the earlier startup-handoff, wrapper-progress, and startup-window bypass fixes. But the session still froze immediately afterward, and the watchdog dump again put the present thread inside `sl_dlss_g` / `sl_interposer` throwing the same threading/mutex family. The key difference from the earlier pre-submit stalls is that the decisive callback had already bypassed both the old startup-window ECL deferral and the first-epoch PostSL warm-up, so the very first PostSL callback after synthetic startup activation immediately submitted real overlay GPU work on the DLSS FG-owned path.
- **Additional issue found in code review**: During deferred startup-window OFF churn, `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` was keeping `DXGIShared::g_StreamlineFGRunning` latched ON but still feeding the raw inactive state into `FGCompatibility`. That let `g_FGCompat.GetRuntimeMode()` collapse to `STREAMLINE_NO_FG` while the live Streamline signal was intentionally still ON, creating an inconsistent state for `IsActualFrameGenerationActive()` and other runtime-mode-based routing decisions during the most fragile startup window.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/streamline_runtime_policy.h` now exposes `ResolveCombinedRuntimeSignalUpdate()` so the deferred-startup-window runtime-state behavior is explicit and testable.
  2. `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` now applies the effective deferred signal state consistently to both `SetStreamlineFGSignal()` and `ApplyCombinedDLSSFGState()`, so the effective runtime mode and multiplier stay coherent while OFF is deferred during startup churn.
  3. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now keeps `ShouldBypassPostSLReactivationWarmupAfterTopLevelHandoffWrapperProgress()` conservative again for pure DLSS cold start, so the first PostSL GPU submit still waits for the established first-epoch warm-up instead of landing on the very first callback.
  4. `tests/test_streamline_runtime_policy.cpp` now covers the deferred startup-window signal/multiplier behavior, and `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now reflects the restored conservative warm-up policy.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline runtime that defers OFF churn during startup needs the effective signal, runtime mode, and multiplier to stay aligned while the deferral is active. And any runtime whose first decisive PostSL callback proves the handoff exists but not yet that the internal FG pipeline is fully stable can still require the existing first-epoch warm-up before the first real overlay ECL submit.

### 2026-04-12 - Restrict late startup-handoff live Present promotion to the game present thread and repair WER API lookup
- **Root cause refinement**: In the follow-up GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_191803`, the earlier "late startup-handoff Present should be allowed through the top-level path" fix clearly removed the old freeze signature: top-level `DetourPresent` traffic resumed immediately after `OFF->ON via SetOptions`, wrapper-queue ECL traffic kept moving, and `Present resumed after 1 stalled frames` appeared instead of the old watchdog freeze buildup. But the bundle crashed almost immediately instead. The hook log shows the new path was too broad: after the first late large-gap Present on the original present thread `0x16CC`, CE also promoted a burst of later Streamline-originated Presents on worker thread `0x55C0` to the top-level live path because `presentOwner=0` was still true between those standalone calls. That turned a narrow startup-handoff escape hatch into a multi-thread top-level routing path. Separately, the same session produced no `crash.log`, no dump file, and no `crash_error.txt`. Source inspection showed one clear dump-path bug: `common/crash_handler.cpp` was looking up `WerRegisterFile` and `WerAddNamedDumpStore` on `kernel32.dll` even though those WER APIs live in `wer.dll`, leaving the programmatic WER fallback path inert.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` now requires one more condition before a Streamline-originated startup-handoff Present can escape the synthetic route: the call must be on the expected game-present thread.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` now compares the current thread against `g_RenderWatchdog.GetMonitoredThreadId()` in both `DetourPresent` and `DetourPresent1`, and only logs/promotes the late startup-handoff Present to the top-level live path when that expected-thread check passes.
  3. `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now covers the wrong-thread case explicitly so a worker-thread startup-handoff Present remains synthetic even if the startup window is active, a recent large Present gap exists, and no other Present currently owns the path.
  4. `common/crash_handler.cpp` now resolves `WerRegisterFile` and `WerAddNamedDumpStore` from `wer.dll` instead of `kernel32.dll`, so the programmatic WER fallback path is at least wired to the correct module when VEH/UEF does not catch the failure.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline runtime can expose a late startup-handoff Present on the actual game-present thread and then immediately emit more standalone worker-thread Presents before the routing settles. The correct distinction is not just "recent large gap" or "no current Present owner"; it is also whether the call is still on the known game-present thread. The WER lookup bug is likewise generic and would silently weaken crash dump capture for any process using this crash handler.

### 2026-04-12 - Treat late Streamline startup-handoff Presents as top-level live Presents after large gaps
- **Root cause refinement**: In the GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_183917`, CE got past the earlier pure-DLSS startup activation issues and reached the later authoritative Streamline runtime-owned handoff plus the real `Streamline Hook: FG state transition OFF->ON via SetOptions`. But the next step still diverged from Talos. GTA exposed exactly one Streamline-originated `DetourPresent` entry after a roughly 390 ms top-level Present gap while the Streamline startup handoff window was still active, then CE saw only continuing `DX12: ECL captured SL wrapper queue ...` traffic before `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames`, and the dump again landed in the same `sl_dlss_g` / `sl_interposer` threading-mutex family. The source-level issue was that `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` still classified every Streamline-originated DX12 Present as synthetic re-entrant whenever `g_StreamlineFGRunning` was true. That is correct for Talos-style recursive/worker-thread Streamline Presents, but it is too broad for a late startup-handoff Present that arrives only after the game has already gone quiet on top-level Present for hundreds of milliseconds.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h` now lets `ShouldTreatStreamlinePresentAsSyntheticReentrant()` consider three extra signals: whether a Streamline startup handoff/window is still in progress, whether another Present currently owns the path, and whether CE recently observed a large top-level Present gap.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` now uses those signals in both `DetourPresent` and `DetourPresent1`. During a late Streamline startup handoff, a Streamline-originated Present that arrives after a recent large top-level Present gap and with no current Present owner is treated as the top-level live Present instead of being forced down the synthetic bypass path.
  3. `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now covers both sides of that policy seam: the existing synthetic-routing cases that must stay synthetic, and the late startup-handoff large-gap case that must now fall through the normal top-level path.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not a GTA-only workaround. Any Streamline runtime can expose a short recursive synthetic Present burst in one title and a later standalone startup-handoff Present in another. The correct routing distinction is not the executable name or even caller identity alone; it is whether CE is still in a startup handoff and whether the presented call looks like the first live top-level Present after the game stopped issuing top-level Presents for a while.
- **Verification**:
  - Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 543 tests passed.
  - Added `DXGISharedTest.StreamlineStartupHandoffPresentUsesTopLevelPathAfterLargeGapWithoutPresentOwner`.

### 2026-04-12 - Pure DLSS cold-start layered stabilization for PostSL activation timing
- **Root cause**: In `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_181616`, GTA V Enhanced DLSS FG froze when activating from cold start (no prior FSR FG). The crash dump lands inside `sl_dlss_g` throwing `std::_Throw_C_error` (threading/mutex). PostSL rendered its first ECL on the SL-owned swapchain queue just 149ms after DLSS FG activation. Four layered timing gaps: (1) `ShouldDelaySyntheticPostSLActivationBehindRepeatedCallbacks(hadFSR=false)` returned false, bypassing 60-frame cooldown to 0; (2) `s_reactivationEpoch > 1` gate skipped first-epoch warmup; (3) startup transition window not re-armed on churn re-activation; (4) no rendering gate during startup window.
- **Fix**: Four layered corrections in `dx12_hook.cpp` and `dx12_overlay_policy.h`:
  1. 15-frame cold-start warmup for first activation epoch (`kPostSLColdStartWarmup`).
  2. Reduced minimum cooldown of 8 for pure DLSS instead of bypass to 0 (`kPureDLSSMinCooldown`).
  3. Re-arm startup transition window + lifecycle reset on FG churn re-activation (ON when callback already installed).
  4. New policy `ShouldDeferPostSLRenderingDuringStartupTransitionWindow` gates ECL submission until window expires or PostSL confirmed rendering.
- **Verification**: Build succeeded, 542 tests passed (new test `DXGISharedTest.PostSLRenderingDeferredDuringStartupTransitionWindowUntilConfirmed` added).

### 2026-04-12 - Prefer direct/original ECL submission on pure-DLSS PostSL swapchain queues once that path is proven
- **Root cause refinement**: In the next GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_180513`, the earlier startup-window lifetime fix was clearly doing its job: CE again reached `PostSL synthetic startup activation complete`, `PostSL REACTIVATED`, and `Post-SL overlay SUBMIT #1`, and this time the immediate transient OFF right after that first submit was correctly deferred by the startup transition window instead of triggering a full OFF teardown. But the session still froze anyway. The remaining signature was: one successful PostSL frame on the live swapchain queue `000002A68BE59550`, continuing wrapper-queue `ECL captured` traffic, then `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames` while the dump again landed in the same Streamline/`sl_dlss_g` threading-mutex family. The important source-level clue was already in CE's own PostSL diagnostic log for that first frame: `queue=... scQueue=... origECL=... realECL=... match=1 sameQueue=1 slWrapper=0 hadFSR=0`, followed immediately by `DX12: PostSL scQueue submit #0 ... virtualCall=1`. In other words, the selected live pure-DLSS swapchain queue had already resolved to the real/original D3D12 ECL entrypoint, but CE still chose the older virtual `scQueue->ExecuteCommandLists(...)` path instead of using the already-proven direct/original ECL submit path.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldUseSelectedSwapchainQueueDirectSubmitForPureDLSS()`.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now prefers the direct/original ECL submit path for pure-DLSS PostSL when all of these are true: no FSR history, the selected queue is the live swapchain queue, and that queue's captured original ECL already matches the real D3D12 ECL entrypoint. The older virtual `scQueue->ExecuteCommandLists(...)` path remains as the fallback for the other pure-DLSS cases where that stronger proof is not yet present.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any pure-DLSS Streamline runtime can move onto a swapchain queue whose current virtual dispatch remains fragile for post-activation Present routing, even after CE has already proven that the queue's captured original ECL path is the real D3D12 entrypoint. Once that stronger proof exists, continuing to force virtual dispatch just re-enters more hook/runtime machinery than necessary.

### 2026-04-12 - Keep Streamline startup-churn protection alive through the first confirmed PostSL startup frame
- **Root cause refinement**: In the next GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_175741`, the prior pure-DLSS startup fix was clearly helping: CE now reached `DX12: PostSL synthetic startup activation complete`, `DX12: PostSL REACTIVATED`, and `Post-SL overlay SUBMIT #1` on the authoritative Streamline swapchain queue `000001B80046A480`, and `perf_metrics_6748.csv` now contained real rows instead of only the header. But the session still froze shortly after. The log shows an immediate transient `DX12: Streamline FG OFF` / `Streamline Hook: FG state transition ON->OFF via GetState` right after that first successful PostSL submit, followed by a second `OFF->ON via SetOptions`, then only wrapper-queue `ECL captured` traffic before `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames`. The source-level issue was that `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` was clearing the Streamline startup transition window as soon as synthetic PostSL activation completed, before the freshly activated PostSL path had proved it could survive even one more startup-churn edge. That let the very next transient OFF escape the dormant startup path and trigger the full non-startup PostSL teardown.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldClearStreamlineStartupTransitionWindowAfterConfirmedPostSLRendering()`.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` no longer clears the startup transition window at synthetic PostSL activation time. Instead, after PostSL confirms rendering and increments its stable-frame counter, CE clears the startup window only once that startup PostSL path has stayed stable long enough to get past the first re-entrant frame.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG startup can still bounce `ON->OFF->ON` immediately after the first successful PostSL startup submit while the runtime finishes internal initialization. Treating "first successful PostSL frame" as equivalent to "startup churn is fully over" is too optimistic.
- **Verification**:

### 2026-04-12 - Unstrand pure-DLSS PostSL startup activation and make perf CSV rows durable in hang sessions
- **Root cause refinement**: In the newer GTA V Enhanced bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_172854`, CE still reached the later authoritative Streamline runtime-owned swapchain (`CreateSwapChainForHwnd INLINE` on queue `000001D40D197310`), suppressed the early `GetState` activation, logged the real `Streamline Hook: FG state transition OFF->ON via SetOptions`, and emitted one synthetic Streamline re-entrant Present. But unlike the Talos comparison bundle, GTA never logged `DX12: PostSL synthetic startup activation complete`, `DX12: PostSL REACTIVATED`, or any `Post-SL overlay SUBMIT` lines afterward. The log only showed continuing `DX12: ECL captured SL wrapper queue ...` entries, then `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames`, and the dump again landed in `sl_dlss_g` / `sl.interposer` throwing the same threading/mutex family. That exposed two remaining CE gaps: the dormant synthetic-startup path was still counting down activation by callback count even on pure-DLSS startup paths where this runtime surfaced only one short synthetic Present burst before switching to its live Present path, leaving PostSL permanently half-armed; and the DXGI refresh path still only repaired the already-tracked vtable anchor, so if Streamline's later live swapchain path moved onto a different Present/Present1 vtable after activation, CE could keep "repairing" the old anchor while top-level Present traffic silently disappeared. Separately, the same bundle's `perf_metrics_1652.csv` contained only the header row. The code-level issue there was durability rather than frame accounting alone: DX12 frame rows are buffered through `hook/common/perf_logger.cpp`, and a hang/crash session can end before stdio flushes the buffered rows to disk.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldDelaySyntheticPostSLActivationBehindRepeatedCallbacks()`, and `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` uses it so the synthetic-startup callback countdown is preserved only for the post-FSR DLSS recovery family. Pure DLSS startup handoffs now clear the callback countdown immediately and let the first dormant synthetic Present promote PostSL instead of requiring many later callbacks that may never arrive.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp/.h` now expose `ShouldRefreshLivePresentHooksForSwapchainPath()` and proactively re-run the Present/Present1 hook refresh from the synthetic/re-entrant Streamline Present paths whenever the currently presented swapchain points at a different vtable anchor or has lost either detour slot. This keeps CE attached to later runtime-created live Present paths instead of only repairing the original anchor.
  3. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` now centralizes the DXGI perf-metrics/published-FG update in a helper shared by both `DetourPresent` and `DetourPresent1`, so sessions dominated by `Present1` keep updating the runtime metrics snapshot too.
  4. `hook/common/perf_logger.h/.cpp` now flush the per-process `perf_metrics_*.csv` file aggressively for the early frames and periodically thereafter, so hang/crash bundles preserve recent rows even when the process never reaches clean shutdown.
- **Why this is generic**: None of this is GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime can expose only a very short synthetic Present burst before switching onto its steady-state live Present path, and any later runtime-created swapchain can move Present/Present1 execution onto a different vtable anchor than the startup path. Likewise, any buffered CSV logger can lose useful per-frame data when a process hangs or crashes before a clean flush.
- **Verification**:

  - Added `DXGISharedTest.LivePresentHookPathRefreshesWhenCurrentSwapchainPathDiffersOrLostHooks`.
  - Added `PerfLoggerTest.PerfMetricsCsvFlushPolicyKeepsEarlyAndPeriodicFramesDurable`.

### 2026-04-12 - Preserve authoritative Streamline runtime queue identity across duplicate create-swapchain capture paths
- **Root cause refinement**: The later GTA V Enhanced freeze bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_165947` still showed the same overall DLSS FG hang family even after the earlier startup-window, queue-hook, and Present-refresh fixes were present in source: a fresh authoritative `CreateSwapChainForHwnd INLINE` handoff onto queue `000001DA75B43300`, one synthetic Streamline re-entrant Present, continued `DX12: ECL captured SL wrapper queue ...`, then `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames`, with the dump again landing in `sl_dlss_g` throwing the same threading/mutex error. The important source-level gap was subtler than the prior fixes: queue capture was still recomputing Streamline/FFX caller authority after the original `CreateSwapChain*` call returned. That let one transient boolean simultaneously mean both “this non-origGame queue belongs to an authoritative Streamline runtime” and “this specific observation is a fresh handoff edge.” Duplicate inline/global capture of the same runtime queue could therefore preserve the correct queue pointer while losing the stronger authoritative classification that should keep the queue hookable, even though startup-window re-arm itself should remain edge-based.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now threads explicit `CreateSwapchainQueueCaptureEvidence` from the deep/inline/global create-swapchain entrypoints into `CaptureSwapchainQueueFromCreateDevice()` instead of re-deriving caller authority after the call returns.
  2. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now separates `ShouldTreatSwapchainQueueAsAuthoritativeStreamlineRuntime()` from `ShouldArmStreamlineStartupTransitionWindowForFreshAuthoritativeRuntimeQueue()`, so authoritative Streamline queue hookability persists across duplicate capture paths while startup-window re-arm still only happens on a fresh queue handoff edge.
  3. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now logs `DX12: Refreshing Present hook path via ...` at every real-swapchain Present refresh site, making future traces unambiguous about whether the refresh path actually ran on the critical handoff.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not GTA-specific. Any runtime that reaches the same real swapchain through more than one interception layer can observe the same queue multiple times during one ownership transition. Queue identity and handoff-edge identity are related but not equivalent, and collapsing them into one transient signal can silently weaken later FG-runtime routing decisions.
- **Verification**:

  - Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 536 tests passed.
  - Added `DXGISharedTest.StreamlineRuntimeQueueAuthorityIsSeparateFromFreshHandoffState`.

### 2026-04-12 - Limit Streamline startup-window re-arm to fresh activation edges and restore authoritative DLSS runtime queue tracking
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_162416`, GTA V Enhanced DLSS FG still froze after the earlier startup-churn fixes. The run reached the real `Streamline Hook: FG state transition OFF->ON via SetOptions` on a later runtime-owned swapchain created on a different D3D12 device (`00000162004AD020` vs original queue/device path `0000015EFEC89DD0`), emitted one synthetic Streamline re-entrant Present, kept logging `DX12: ECL captured SL wrapper queue ...`, then hit `Present STALLED for 10/30 frames` and dumped with the game's present thread again inside `sl_dlss_g` throwing a C++ threading/mutex error. Two CE issues overlapped: `DX12_SetSwapchainQueue()` was still blanket-skipping the ExecuteCommandLists vtable hook for all non-origGame runtime-owned queues (`preserving FSR timing`), which protects native FSR but also left authoritative Streamline runtime queues without direct ECL visibility; and `ApplyCombinedStreamlineRuntimeState(true)` / `DX12_OnStreamlineFGStateChanged(true)` were re-arming the Streamline startup transition window on later active DLSS polls, so the startup-only OFF-deferral logic could stay alive deep into normal runtime operation instead of expiring after the fresh handoff.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/streamline_runtime_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldArmStartupTransitionWindowOnFreshActiveSignal()`, and `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` only re-arms the startup transition window on fresh active edges (`active && !previousSignal`) instead of every later active state poll.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` no longer re-arms the startup transition window again inside `DX12_OnStreamlineFGStateChanged(true)`; authoritative create-swapchain handoffs remain the primary startup-window source, and fresh `SetOptions` activation only preserves that window through the immediate churn rather than extending it indefinitely.
  3. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now exposes `ShouldHookSwapchainQueueVTableForFrameGenerationRuntime()`, and `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` uses it so generic runtime-owned queues still stay unhooked for native FSR timing safety, but authoritative Streamline runtime-owned swapchain queues are hooked for ExecuteCommandLists tracking.
  4. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now centralizes real-swapchain Present refresh through `RefreshPresentHooksForRealSwapchain()` and uses it in the deep, inline, global `CreateSwapChain`, and global `CreateSwapChainForHwnd` success paths so later runtime-created swapchains refresh Present interception consistently.
- **Why this is generic**: The problem is not GTA-specific. Any Streamline DLSS FG runtime can create a later authoritative swapchain/queue on a different device after startup, and any startup-only transition window that is refreshed by steady-state active polls can accidentally keep startup suppression rules alive into normal runtime operation. Likewise, native FSR timing constraints should not force CE to give up all authoritative runtime-queue tracking for non-FSR DLSS handoffs.
- **Verification**:

  - Added `StreamlineRuntimePolicyTest.StartupTransitionWindowOnlyRearmsOnFreshActiveSignal`.
  - Added `DXGISharedTest.AuthoritativeStreamlineRuntimeQueuesStayHookableWhileGenericRuntimeQueuesDoNot`.

### 2026-04-12 - Add selectable logging levels, compact session manifests, and token-efficient wiki routing
- **Change**:
  1. `config.ini` now supports `log_level=off|error|warn|info|debug|trace` as the primary logging control, while keeping `debug_logging` and `perf_metrics_logging` as compatibility inputs.
  2. `debug` remains the intended normal debugging level. `trace` is now the explicitly more verbose forensic tier for byte dumps, per-instruction hook traces, and the per-frame perf CSV logger.
  3. Session bundles now gain a compact `session_manifest.txt` entrypoint so readers and agents can orient themselves before opening the detailed logs.
  4. `captureengine/sensor_service.cpp` now logs change events plus periodic summaries instead of a repeated every-second steady-state update line.
  5. `hook/wrappers/inline_hook.cpp` now keeps the byte-dump / per-instruction tracing on the new `trace` tier while leaving the higher-value install/failure lines in normal debug logs.
  6. Added `llm-wiki/current.md` and updated `index.md` to route readers through a compact current-state page before the long historical archive in `log.md`.
- **Why this helps without losing debuggability**: The new split keeps the routine state-transition and failure logs easy to read in `debug`, while still preserving the forensic detail under `trace` when deeper hook or timing analysis is needed. The wiki routing change preserves historical genesis in `log.md` and the topical pages instead of deleting it, but reduces the need to re-read the full archive for every task.

### 2026-04-12 - Defer g_StreamlineFGRunning OFF signal during startup transition window to prevent multi-device DLSS FG threading race
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_151606`, GTA V Enhanced DLSS FG still froze after the dormant-callback fix from `20260412_142710`. The freeze dump shows the same pattern: game's present thread stuck inside `sl_dlss_g` throwing `std::_Throw_C_error` (C++ threading/mutex error). The key sequence was: DLSS FG activates via SetOptions (first ON), then GetState reports OFF during initialization (transient signal drop), and `g_StreamlineFGRunning` is immediately set to `false`, toggling CE's Present routing. DLSS FG re-activates via SetOptions (second ON), `g_StreamlineFGRunning` goes back to `true`. This toggling during SL's fragile multi-device initialization (third swapchain on a different D3D12 device) changes timing enough to expose a threading race in `sl_dlss_g`. Present traffic stopped permanently after the first synthetic re-entrant Present, and the game thread eventually crashed inside SL.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` `ApplyCombinedStreamlineRuntimeState(false)` now checks `IsStreamlineStartupTransitionWindowActive()`. When the startup window is active and the signal is OFF, `g_StreamlineFGRunning` keeps its current value (true) instead of being set to false. The `DX12_OnStreamlineFGStateChanged` callback is also suppressed during this deferral. After the window expires, the next GetState or SetOptions call processes the real state normally.
- **Why this is generic**: Any DX12 game that uses DLSS FG through Streamline on a multi-device configuration can experience transient GetState signal drops during initialization. The fix keeps CE's Present routing stable throughout the churn, avoiding timing changes that can trigger threading races in SL's internal state machine.
- **Verification**:
  - Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 531 tests passed.
- Pages touched: `frame-generation-switching.md`, `log.md`.
- Source files checked: `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_151606/hook_debug.log`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_151606/GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-12_15-17-46_638.dmp`, `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp`, `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp`, `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp`.
- Stale-risk note: Re-check this family whenever DLSS FG startup timing, `g_StreamlineFGRunning` signal propagation, or multi-device overlay initialization changes. If a future bundle again shows `OFF->ON` churn followed by `sl_dlss_g` threading crash, check whether the startup-window deferral is still working correctly.

### 2026-04-12 - Keep PostSL callback dormant during DLSS FG startup churn to prevent freeze
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_142710`, GTA V Enhanced DLSS FG still froze after the earlier fixes. The freeze dump shows the game's present thread stuck inside `sl_dlss_g` throwing `std::_Throw_Cpp_error` (a C++ standard library threading/mutex error). The key sequence was: DLSS FG activates via SetOptions (first ON), GetState reports OFF (transient signal drop during initialization), CE fully tears down the PostSL callback and lifecycle state, then DLSS FG re-activates via SetOptions (second ON), CE re-installs and re-initializes everything from scratch. This repeated full teardown/re-init cycle on every OFF/ON transition during the startup transition window — including clearing the callback pointer, waiting for in-flight callbacks to drain, resetting the lifecycle epoch, and clearing cached queue state — interfered with DLSS FG's fragile multi-device initialization (new swapchain on a different D3D12 device `0000019D3E6D7670` vs the game's original `0000019A02992040`).
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` `DX12_OnStreamlineFGStateChanged(false)` now checks `IsStreamlineStartupTransitionWindowActive()`. When DLSS FG goes OFF during the startup window, CE keeps the PostSL callback pointer installed but disables execution (`g_PostSLCallbackExecutionEnabled = false`), and returns early without draining queues, waiting for in-flight callbacks, or resetting the lifecycle epoch.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` `DX12_OnStreamlineFGStateChanged(true)` now checks if the PostSL callback is already installed (churn re-activation). If so, it just re-enables execution without re-installing or calling `ResetPostSLLifecycleForTransition`, avoiding the full teardown/re-init cycle.
- **Why this is generic**: Any DX12 game that uses DLSS FG through Streamline can experience transient GetState signal drops during FG initialization, especially on multi-device configurations where the FG runtime creates its own D3D12 device and swapchain. The fix keeps CE's PostSL state machine stable through these transient drops instead of treating each one as a full teardown/re-init cycle.
- **Verification**:
  - Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 531 tests passed.
- Pages touched: `frame-generation-switching.md`, `log.md`.
- Source files checked: `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_142710/hook_debug.log`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_142710/GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-12_14-28-54_777.dmp`, `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp`, `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp`, `hook/common/dxgi_shared.h`.
- Stale-risk note: Re-check this family whenever DLSS FG startup timing, PostSL callback lifecycle management, or multi-device overlay initialization changes. If a future bundle again shows `OFF->ON via GetState` during startup followed by DLSS FG crashing inside `sl_dlss_g`, check whether the startup-churn dormant-callback path is still working correctly.

### 2026-04-12 - Refresh Present hooks for inline Streamline swapchain handoffs
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_135934`, GTA V Enhanced got past the earlier wrapped-startup classification bug and the premature `GetState` activation race. The hook log shows a second authoritative Streamline runtime-owned swapchain handoff at `14:01:07.050-14:01:07.087`, then a real `Streamline Hook: FG state transition OFF->ON via SetOptions` at `14:01:08.654`, but top-level `DetourPresent` traffic never resumed after the first synthetic Streamline re-entrant Present while `DX12: ECL captured SL wrapper queue ...` entries kept appearing. The freeze dump `GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-12_14-01-19_400.dmp` again captured the target present thread inside `sl_dlss_g`, which fits Streamline running on the new swapchain path while CE no longer saw the top-level Present entrypoint for that swapchain.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now refreshes Present hooks and runs `RepairVTableHooksIfNeeded()` in the inline `CreateSwapChainForHwnd` success path before queue capture, matching the deep/global create-swapchain paths.
  2. This keeps CE's top-level Present detour path aligned when a later Streamline-created DX12 swapchain exposes a different Present implementation or vtable than the startup swapchain.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not a GTA-only workaround. Any DX12 runtime that creates a later real swapchain on a different Present implementation path can strand a detour that was only installed against the startup swapchain's Present implementation. The correct invariant is that every newly created real swapchain path must refresh Present interception consistently, regardless of whether CE saw it via the global, deep, or inline create-swapchain hook.

### 2026-04-12 - Suppress premature GetState-only DLSS activation during authoritative Streamline startup handoff
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_133431`, GTA V Enhanced no longer died on the earlier EOS-wrapped create-swapchain classification issue, but DLSS FG startup still churned `OFF->ON->OFF->ON` and then froze. The hook log shows a new authoritative Streamline runtime-owned swapchain handoff on queue `0000018E0CB31730`, then an immediate `Streamline Hook: FG state transition OFF->ON via GetState`, a synthetic Streamline re-entrant Present, `ON->OFF via GetState`, and finally the explicit `OFF->ON via SetOptions` activation. The dump `GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-12_13-36-13_500.dmp` captured the target thread down inside `sl_dlss_g` throwing a C++ exception, which fits a bad startup activation race rather than another self-targeted watchdog freeze.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now arms the existing Streamline startup transition window whenever CE observes a fresh authoritative Streamline runtime-owned swapchain handoff on a new non-game queue.
  2. `hook/common/streamline_runtime_policy.h` now exposes a helper for suppressing fresh `GetState`-only DLSS activation while the runtime still reports `Off` / `StreamlineNoFG` and either the explicit SetOptions block or the startup transition window is active.
  3. `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` now uses that helper in `ShouldSuppressNewGetStateActivation()` and logs whether a suppressed fresh activation is being blocked by the startup transition window, the persistent SetOptions block, or both.
  4. `tests/test_streamline_runtime_policy.cpp` now covers the new startup-transition suppression rule so future changes do not silently re-allow `GetState` to win the race during startup handoff.
- **Why this is generic**: This is not a GTA-only special case. Any Streamline startup path that first exposes a new runtime-owned swapchain/queue and only later issues the explicit `slDLSSGSetOptions` enable can transiently report enough `GetState` evidence to look active too early. CE should key suppression off the observed startup handoff and runtime state, not off executable names.

### 2026-04-12 - Archive session-local CE symbols and upgrade direct dump richness with compatibility fallbacks
- **Gap addressed**: Even after adding native PDB emission, later dump analysis still depended on the current installed binaries matching the original crash session. Also, CE's direct `MiniDumpWriteDump` paths were still using relatively lean dump flag sets compared with what is useful for real crash and hang diagnosis.
- **Change**:
  1. `common/crash_handler.cpp` now archives the current CE runtime PE/PDB set into `<dump-dir>\symbols\captureengine\` whenever the crash dump directory is set, so session dumps can be analyzed against a stable local symbol snapshot later.
  2. `common/crash_dump_policy.h` now centralizes richer direct-dump flag sets for crash, assert, and freeze paths while explicitly keeping `MiniDumpWithFullMemory` out of the always-on direct dump path.
  3. `common/crash_handler.cpp` now tries a richer primary dump first, then a compatibility dump, then normal fallback forms if the richer combination is rejected in a particular target process.
  4. `hook/common/freeze_watchdog.cpp` now uses the same richer-first / compatibility-fallback pattern for watchdog-triggered freeze dumps instead of a single fixed lean dump type.
  5. `analysis/analyze_dump.ps1` now prefers dump-local archived symbols under the dump directory before falling back to the repo's current installed outputs.
- **Why this is generic**: The need to preserve exact matching symbols and to capture more thread/module/process metadata is not game-specific. Any later rebuild can invalidate repo-local symbol matching for an older dump, and any crash or hang investigation benefits from thread info, unloaded modules, handle data, and full-memory metadata even when full raw process memory is intentionally not captured.
- **Verification**:
  - Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 530 tests passed.
  - Added `tests/test_crash_dump_policy.cpp` coverage for the new richer dump flag sets and the runtime-artifact archive filter.
  - Verified `analysis/analyze_dump.ps1` now includes `<dump-dir>\symbols\captureengine` ahead of repo-local symbol directories.

- Source files checked: `common/crash_handler.cpp`, `common/crash_handler.h`, `common/crash_dump_policy.h`, `hook/common/freeze_watchdog.cpp`, `analysis/analyze_dump.ps1`, `tests/test_crash_dump_policy.cpp`.

### 2026-04-12 - Fix self-targeted automatic stall dumps and repeated late-startup re-arm churn in GTA DLSS FG freeze path
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_034953`, GTA V Enhanced again reached a late pre-FG runtime-owned Streamline handoff, but CE's startup-compat re-arm logic was still level-triggered on `runtimeOwnsSwapchain && startupCompatSettled` rather than edge-triggered on the new handoff itself. Once the original game queue path resumed, `ProcessFrame` immediately re-armed startup compatibility again on the next frame because the runtime-owned swapchain still existed, producing thousands of repeated `Re-arming startup overlay compatibility...` / `Startup overlay probe complete - rendering stably` cycles all the way until DLSS activation. When DLSS finally turned on at `03:51:24`, CE saw `OFF->ON->OFF->ON` churn and then `Streamline Hook: Present STALLED` warnings. The later automatic dump `GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-12_03-52-09_514.dmp` showed the supposedly stalled present thread `T:4784` sitting inside CE (`capture_hook_x64!DX12_WaitForOverlayCompletion`) while dump capture was in progress. That happened because the 30-frame stall path requested an immediate dump targeting the current thread and executed it synchronously on that same thread. Separately, stall detection was only watching `DetourPresent` traffic, so any top-level `Present1` traffic during DLSS activation would look like a false stall even if the runtime was still making progress.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` and `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now treat late pre-FG startup re-arm as a one-shot per newly observed runtime-owned handoff instead of re-triggering on every later frame while the runtime-owned swapchain remains present.
  2. `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp/.h` now count both `DetourPresent` and `DetourPresent1` as top-level present progress for Streamline stall detection.
  3. `hook/common/freeze_watchdog.h/.cpp` now defer immediate dump requests to the watchdog thread when the caller thread already matches the requested dump target, avoiding self-suspension/self-capture on the render/present thread.
  4. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now aligns the watchdog's monitored thread with the live non-DLSS game present thread once that thread is known, so later freeze-triggered dumps target the actual render thread instead of the startup hook thread.
- **Why this is generic**: None of these failures are GTA-specific. Any startup-blocking overlay stack can leave a runtime-owned swapchain live long enough for a level-triggered startup re-arm condition to flap indefinitely. Any DXGI runtime can switch between `Present` and `Present1`. Any synchronous dump path that targets the current render thread can freeze the game while trying to diagnose a suspected stall.
- **Verification**: Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 527 tests passed.
- Pages touched: `dx12-overlay-third-party-coexistence.md`, `frame-generation-switching.md`, `regression-testing-and-logging.md`, `log.md`.

- Stale-risk note: Re-check this family whenever startup re-arm triggering, DXGI `Present`/`Present1` accounting, or watchdog immediate-dump behavior changes. If a future bundle again shows `Present STALLED` followed by an automatic dump that lands inside CE's own dump/wait path, revisit the self-targeted dump deferral first.

### 2026-04-12 - Fix late pre-DLSS runtime-owned startup handoff leaving overlay invisible and tighten automatic hang dump targeting
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_023846`, CE correctly re-armed startup compatibility after a late pre-FG runtime-owned handoff (`CreateSwapChain Global Streamline fallback` captured queue `000001C698D8F900` at `02:39:23.981`), but it still treated startup compatibility as requiring runtime ownership to disappear entirely before overlay rendering could resume. The runtime-owned non-FSR queue remained live and stable for nearly a minute before DLSS FG activation, so the overlay never came back even though the handoff had already settled enough to render safely. Later, DLSS activation churned `OFF->ON->OFF->ON`, `Present STALLED` warnings appeared, and the manual dump captured at `02:40:46` showed that the automatic dump path had not fired early enough to preserve the pre-dump hang state. Separately, the watchdog design was vulnerable to helper Present/ECL heartbeats rewriting the monitored thread ID, so an automatic dump could easily target the wrong thread once helper traffic took over.
- **Fix**:
  1. `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now keeps startup compatibility logically active through a settled late pre-FG runtime-owned handoff, but allows rendering again once that runtime-owned queue has remained stable for the configured settle window.
  2. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now routes that settled pre-FG runtime-owned startup window back through the proven original game queue instead of continuing to trust the not-yet-normal runtime-owned queue as the default pre-DLSS render path.
  3. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` also clears stale Streamline-off teardown grace when a fresh DLSS activation begins, so the next `ON` transition does not keep ignoring newly resurfacing wrapper/direct queues from the previous `OFF` transition.
  4. `hook/common/freeze_watchdog.h/.cpp`, `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp`, `hook/wrappers/dxgi_swapchain_wrap.cpp`, and `hook/apis/streamline_hook.cpp` now separate helper-thread heartbeats from monitored-thread ownership, let DX12 provide the preferred game-present thread for dump capture, and request an immediate automatic dump when `Present STALLED` reaches the sustained 30-frame threshold.
- **Why this is generic**: The bug is not GTA-specific. Any startup-blocking overlay stack can hand the live swapchain to a runtime-owned queue before real FG starts and then keep that runtime-owned topology alive for a while before FG truly activates. Likewise, any watchdog that lets helper traffic retarget its monitored thread can miss or mis-capture later hangs once rendering work migrates across threads.
- **Verification**: Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates`. Build completed successfully and all 526 tests passed.
- Pages touched: `dx12-overlay-third-party-coexistence.md`, `frame-generation-switching.md`, `regression-testing-and-logging.md`, `log.md`.

### 2026-04-12 - Fix GTA startup ERR_GFX_STATE caused by late pre-FG runtime-owned swapchain handoff after startup compat settled too early
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_022704`, GTA V Enhanced reached stable non-FG startup overlay rendering on the original game queue and CE marked startup overlay compatibility as settled at `02:27:39.567`. About 2.1 seconds later, before any real FG activation had occurred, EOS/Streamline created a later runtime-owned swapchain on queue `0000022199CD6160`. CE captured that queue and recognized it as runtime-owned, but because the earlier first-submit latch had already disabled startup compatibility, `ProcessFrame` immediately ran the normal overlay path on that runtime-owned startup handoff. GTA then raised the modal `ERR_GFX_STATE` dialog and the watchdog dump showed the game sitting in `user32!MessageBoxW` with exception `e0000001` (`Blocking`).
- **Fix**: `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h` now distinguishes "startup compat has settled" from "the session has already seen real FG activity" and exposes a helper that allows startup compatibility to re-arm for a late runtime-owned startup handoff before any real FG has happened. `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now tracks whether any real FG activity has been observed, re-arms startup compatibility when that exact pre-FG handoff appears, resets the staged startup activation state, and only treats later overlay popups as normal coexistence after actual FG activity has occurred.
- **Why this is generic**: The bug is not GTA-specific. Any startup-blocking overlay stack can briefly look settled on the original queue, then still transfer the live swapchain to a runtime-owned queue later in startup. The correct boundary for leaving startup-only safeguards is not "first successful overlay draw" by itself, but "startup settled and no later pre-FG runtime-owned handoff arrived".

### 2026-04-12 - Fix GTA DLSS FG startup misclassification when EOS wraps Streamline swapchain creation
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_014238`, GTA V Enhanced did not produce a dump, but the hook log showed DLSS FG startup entering `OFF->ON->OFF->ON` churn and then stopping after `Streamline Hook: Present STALLED ... vtable hook bypassed?`. The important earlier clue was `CreateSwapChainForHwnd INLINE` / `DetourCreateSwapChainForHwndGlobal` classifying the new swapchain for the real game HWND as an EOS third-party overlay swapchain and bypassing normal CE swapchain side-effects, even though the subsequent ECL traffic showed Streamline-owned queues. CE already lets FFX stack evidence override wrapped EOS/Social Club caller identity for native FSR runtime takeovers, but it did not do the same for Streamline/DLSS FG startup, so GTA's startup-blocking overlay wrapper could strand the new DLSS swapchain on the wrong coexistence path.
- **Fix**: `hook/common/overlay_compat.h`, `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h`, and `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` now recognize Streamline frame-generation modules/stack evidence during create-swapchain classification and treat those wrapped calls as authoritative FG-runtime swapchain creation instead of foreign overlay traffic.
- **Why this is generic**: The bug is not EOS-specific and not GTA-specific. Any startup-blocking overlay that wraps `CreateSwapChain*` can obscure the real FG runtime behind an overlay module return address. The correct signal is the observed FG runtime stack, not the immediate caller alone.

### 2026-04-12 - Fix GTA FSR->DLSS crash caused by unrelocated short branch in FFX destroy trampoline
- **Root cause**: In log bundle `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_011505`, GTA V Enhanced crashed immediately after switching from native FSR FG to DLSS FG. The dump showed a null write at `00007FF8011D031B`, inside CE's inline-hook trampoline pool rather than inside GTA or the GPU driver. Hook logs mapped that address to the `ffxDestroyContext` trampoline (`trampoline=00007FF8011D0300`). That trampoline had copied `test rcx, rcx; jne +0x0B` from `amd_fidelityfx_dx12.dll` byte-for-byte; when the branch was taken during FFX teardown, it jumped into the trampoline's appended `FF 25` jump stub payload instead of the original function body and faulted.
- **Fix**: `hook/wrappers/inline_hook.cpp` now relocates short control transfers that leave the copied block instead of blindly memcpying them. The helper handles short `JMP` and short `Jcc` in the standard inline-hook trampoline path, the external-chain trampoline path, the deep-hook displaced-block trampoline path, and bypass trampolines built from on-disk bytes. Short loop-family opcodes that cannot be generically widened are now rejected explicitly instead of producing a corrupt trampoline.
- **Why this is generic**: The crash surfaced in GTA's `FSR -> DLSS` handoff because that path exercised `ffxDestroyContext()`, but the actual bug was a generic inline-hook trampoline correctness issue. Any hooked function with an external short branch leaving the copied prologue could have crashed similarly.
- **Verification**: Ran `python build.py --incremental --run-tests --skip-updates` after the code change, then reran it after formatting the edited file with the bundled formatter. Both runs completed successfully with `Build Complete.` and `[  PASSED  ] 520 tests.`
- Pages touched: `frame-generation-switching.md`, `log.md`.
- Source files checked: `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_011505/hook_debug.log`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_011505/crash.log`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260412_011505/cdb_analysis.log`, `hook/wrappers/inline_hook.cpp`, `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp`, `hook/apis/ffx_hook.cpp`, `tests/test_stubs.cpp`.
- Regression coverage note: no new focused unit test was added because current unit binaries stub out `InlineHook` in `tests/test_stubs.cpp`, so the real trampoline relocation code is not presently unit-testable through the existing harness. Verification therefore relied on full build + existing unit suite plus crash-log/root-cause analysis.
- Stale-risk note: Any later change to trampoline relocation, slot size assumptions, or FFX hook installation should re-check this crash family, especially if a dump lands in the `00007FF8011D....` trampoline pool again.

### 2026-04-11 - Fix FSR FG + overlay freeze (SL routing re-enablement bug)
- **Root cause**: When FSR FG activated in GTA V Enhanced, `DisableSLPresentRouting()` correctly set `s_slRoutingActive = false`, but `DetectSLPresentHook()` on the next Present call immediately re-enabled it because the SL E9 JMP was still present at `oPresent`. This caused Present to go through SL's hook chain while FSR FG owned the swapchain, deadlocking the render thread inside `amd_fidelityfx_dx12!ffxQuery`.
- **Fix**: Added FSR FG state checks at three layers:
  1. `DetectSLPresentHook()` now refuses to re-enable SL routing when the effective runtime mode is FSR.
  2. DetourPresent SL detection guard skips `DetectSLPresentHook()` when the effective runtime mode is FSR.
  3. DetourPresent1 SL detection guard also skips when the effective runtime mode is FSR.
  4. Safety latch in the Present/Present1 routing paths: if SL routing is somehow active while the effective runtime mode is FSR, force-disable it and route through the trampoline directly.
- **Evidence**: Freeze dump from `GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-11_20-45-30_438.dmp` showed render thread T:3538 stuck in `amd_fidelityfx_dx12!ffxQuery`. Hook debug log showed SL routing DISABLED at 20:44:57.077 then re-ACTIVE at 20:44:57.230 (153ms later), contradicting the FFX takeover.
- Pages touched: `log.md`, `frame-generation-switching.md`.
- Source files checked: `hook/common/dxgi_shared.cpp` (lines 698-742 DetectSLPresentHook, 981-991 Present SL check, 1173-1204 Present routing, 1303-1304 Present1 SL check, 1431-1439 Present1 routing, 2234-2242 DisableSLPresentRouting), `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp` (line 1445 DisableSLPresentRouting call site), `hook/common/fg_detection.h`, `hook/common/fg_runtime_state.h`.
- Regression coverage: `tests/test_fg_runtime_state.cpp` now asserts heuristic FSR classification is treated as FSR for routing guards.
- Stale-risk note: SL routing suppression during FSR FG is now a critical invariant. Any change to SL routing logic or FG runtime classification must verify this invariant holds.

### 2026-04-11 - Suppress injected DX12 overlay GPU work during native FSR FG
- **Root cause**: The earlier SL-routing fix stopped stale Streamline Present routing, but CE still resumed injected DX12 overlay work after authoritative FFX swapchain takeover. In the `20260411_211719` GTA V Enhanced log, CE reinitialized the overlay on the FFX-owned queue and emitted `FG overlay SUBMIT #1..20` on that queue immediately before the render thread later hung again in `amd_fidelityfx_dx12!ffxQuery`.
- **Fix**:
  1. `ShouldSkipSeparateOverlayGpuWorkForRuntimeOwnedFrameGeneration()` now returns true for runtime-owned native FSR states instead of allowing the injected DX12 overlay GPU path to resume on the FFX-owned queue.
  2. `InitOverlaySync()` now honors that policy by returning before creating new fences / allocators / command lists, truly keeping sync resources idle during native FSR ownership.
- **Behavioral result**: CE still tracks/publishes FSR FG state, but it intentionally does not rebuild or submit injected DX12 overlay GPU work while native/runtime-owned FSR owns the swapchain queue. Generic runtime-owned non-FSR windows still stay on the normal recovery path.
- **Evidence**: Freeze dump `GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-11_21-19-25_496.dmp` shows the render thread stuck in `amd_fidelityfx_dx12!ffxQuery`. Hook log shows FFX takeover at 21:18:52.194, followed by `InitOverlaySync` on queue `000001FEF108CDA0` and `FG overlay SUBMIT #1..20` on that same queue before the freeze watchdog fires.
- Pages touched: `frame-generation-switching.md`, `log.md`.
- Source files checked: `hook/common/dx12_overlay_policy.h`, `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp`, `hook/common/fg_detection.cpp`, `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260411_211719/hook_debug.log`, `installed/captureengine/logs/20260411_211719/GTA5_Enhanced.exe_FREEZE_2026-04-11_21-19-25_496.dmp`.
- Regression coverage: `tests/test_dxgi_shared.cpp` now asserts runtime-owned native FSR suppresses injected overlay GPU work while generic runtime-owned non-FSR windows do not.
- Stale-risk note: If native FSR support later grows a safe piggyback or runtime-cooperative overlay path, revisit this suppression rule carefully. Until then, absence of injected `FG overlay SUBMIT` traffic during runtime-owned FSR FG is a safety invariant.

### 2026-04-11 - Restore overlay during native FSR FG via FFX present-callback bridge
- **Root cause**: The previous safety fix correctly stopped the freezing `FG overlay SUBMIT` path on the FFX-owned queue, but it also removed every visible injected DX12 overlay path for native/runtime-owned FSR FG. The transition model still expected a runtime-owned FSR overlay mode, but `ProcessFrame`/`InitOverlaySync` hard-deferred init, sync, and draw work, leaving the overlay permanently invisible whenever native FSR FG was active.
- **Fix**:
  1. Extended `hook/common/ffx_api_parsing.h` to model FFX frame-generation present-callback structures and callback state bits that CE can legally observe.
  2. Hooked `ffxConfigure()` so native FSR FG now installs a CE bridge callback through FFX's own `presentCallback` path.
  3. The bridge preserves/chains the runtime's original present callback (or FFX's default `ffxFrameInterpolationUiComposition` callback when the game provides none), then renders CE's overlay on the FFX-supplied command list and output surface.
  4. CE still keeps the old separate injected `FG overlay SUBMIT` path suppressed for runtime-owned FSR FG, so the previous freeze-triggering queue submission path remains disabled.
- **Behavioral result**: Native FSR FG no longer depends on separate injected queue submits to show the overlay. Overlay composition is moved onto FFX's own present-time composition path, which is the runtime-supported UI handling path described by the upstream SDK.
- **Verification**: `python build.py` completed successfully and the unit suite passed with 520 tests. Added `tests/test_ffx_api_parsing.cpp` coverage for FFX present callback shape and premultiplied-alpha extension parsing.
- Pages touched: `frame-generation-switching.md`, `log.md`.
- Source files checked: `hook/apis/dx12_hook.cpp`, `hook/apis/dx12_hook.h`, `hook/apis/ffx_hook.cpp`, `hook/apis/ffx_hook.h`, `hook/common/ffx_api_parsing.h`, `tests/test_ffx_api_parsing.cpp`, plus upstream FidelityFX SDK headers/docs for `ffxConfigureDescFrameGeneration`, `ffxCallbackDescFrameGenerationPresent`, and DX12 swapchain UI composition.
- Stale-risk note: This path now depends on FFX callback ABI assumptions remaining correct. Any change to native FFX SDK integration, callback chaining, or CE overlay backend state handling must re-check runtime-owned FSR overlay composition carefully in GTA V Enhanced and Talos validation scenarios.
